\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 In Commercial reports, the stored 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  field should be interpreted as Commercial presence / naturalism only where that meaning is tied to observable Commercial evidence. It may cover believable scale, copy communication, task-calibrated tone, camera or reader relationship, addressee logic, clarity of thought, rhythm, immediacy, and product / situation grounding where supplied or visible. It must not become a generic theatre-acting score or a vague \'93presence\'94 score.\cell \row

\cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Generic Commercial phrases are not sufficient unless attached to observable behaviour. Terms such as natural, confident, relatable, authentic, strong presence, engaging, conversational, likeable, warm, friendly, approachable, believable, grounded, clear tone, strong copy read, good camera work, professional self-tape, polished, marketable or bookable must be replaced with what was visible, where it occurred and why it matters: a copy beat, addressee shift, camera relationship, product/situation cue, rhythm change, tone adjustment, reaction, facial/physical communication, speech/audibility condition or assessability limitation.\cell \row

\cf0 In no-brief Commercial reports, do not invent brand, product, campaign, buyer, target audience, casting fit, commercial market, product category, role requirements, campaign world, time limit or platform requirement. Keep the report to what the tape itself shows: copy if audible or visible, performance scale, camera relationship, tone, clarity, timing, assessability and professional standards. If the missing brief limits confidence, state the limitation rather than filling the gap.\cell \row

Direct-to-camera work may address lens relationship, addressee, scale, immediacy, and whether the performer appears to communicate to a specific viewer or buyer only where supported by brief/copy/tape. Off-camera-reader or scene-style Commercial work should assess the performer\'92s relationship to the reader or scene target, controlled near-lens eyeline where appropriate, listening/responding on cue, and copy/situation clarity. Do not apply one universal eye-line or direct-address rule to every Commercial tape.\cell \row

Use only task-present, observable evidence for these contexts. Do not create universal subtype rules, hidden weights, or style expectations for them. If the context appears but the brief is thin, mark it cautiously and keep comments to observable copy, camera, tone, situation and assessability evidence.\cell \row

\cf0 Commercial performance claims should be strongest only when the tape is assessable: the performer can be clearly seen and heard, framing is task-appropriate, eyeline/reader setup is readable, lighting allows facial or physical communication to register, and continuity is sufficient to judge the performance. If visibility, framing, sound or continuity materially limits assessment, narrow the claims and lower feedback reliability rather than substituting generic performance criticism.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Audio comments should distinguish audibility from Commercial performance. Poor sound, room noise, low volume, distortion or unclear capture may limit reliability, but they do not by themselves prove weak copy handling, weak naturalism or weak speech skill. Where spoken copy is audible enough to assess, comments may address clarity, pace, emphasis, rhythm, cue-word handling and tone. Do not treat accent, speech difference, gender-diverse voice, hearing difference or communication support as a Commercial deficit.\cell \row

\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Commercial assessment must separate access process, communication support and performance evidence. Access needs, disability, neurodivergence, visual impairment, Deaf/disabled access, hearing difference, speech difference, accent, gender-diverse voice, mobility difference, convalescence, fatigue, lighting needs, time-slot needs, accessible materials, support workers or adaptation must not be treated as Commercial deficits. Judge only what is observable and assessable in the task. Do not infer capability ceiling, marketability, professionalism, buyer fit or role suitability from access context.\cell \row

\cf0 Commercial scoring and report language must not use marketability, bookability, buyer fit, brand fit, product fit, target-audience fit, commercial look, appearance, body type, attractiveness, charm, charisma, class-coded polish, fame, follower count, social-media profile, influencer status, age appearance, race, gender presentation, disability or other protected characteristics as evidence unless a supplied brief lawfully and specifically supports a narrow factual requirement. \cf0 Proposed revised text\cell 
\pard\intbl\itap1\pardeftab720\partightenfactor0
\cf0 In Commercial reports, professional_presentation should be limited to observable assessability, explicit brief response, safe process and task preparation. It must not reward studio quality, expensive equipment, paid readers, paid editing, paid coaching, home quality, wardrobe cost, grooming taste, class-coded presentation, charm, look, marketability or resource access. Presentation notes should be used only when they materially affect assessability, explicit brief compliance or safe process.\cell \row

\cf0 Commercial timestamped notes, strengths, improvements, fix-first and next-take advice must be tied to observable Commercial evidence. Strong notes should identify the copy beat, camera/addressee relationship, tone adjustment, product/situation cue, reader exchange, no-dialogue reaction, speech/audibility issue, framing/visibility issue or assessability limitation. For assessable Commercial tapes, timestamped notes should cover the key beginning/middle/end moments where useful and should include at least one high-value strength and one improvement where justified. Do not pad with generic notes if the tape is not assessable.\cell \row

\cf0 \strokec2 Commercial feedback must not become theatre-style acting feedback or generic screen-acting feedback. Avoid defaulting to theatrical stakes, extended character psychology, generic \'93screen naturalism\'94 or vague \'93presence\'94 language unless the task or tape supports that context.\
Commercial tone and copy feedback must be grounded in supplied brief, script, product, campaign, situation, visible copy clues or observable tape behaviour. If no brief or material context is supplied, do not invent brand, product, buyer, target audience, campaign world, product category, casting fit, commercial market, hidden brief requirement or platform requirement.\
Direct-to-camera, off-camera-reader, scene-style, no-dialogue and voiceover-style Commercial tasks require different evidence expectations. Use the most specific Commercial context supported by the brief or tape. If the context is unclear, say so and keep the report to observable Commercial evidence.\
Assessability is a precondition for reliable Commercial feedback. Strong Commercial performance claims require clear sight, audible speech or copy, task-appropriate framing, readable eyeline or reader setup, and enough continuity to judge the work. If technical or audio limitations materially reduce assessability, narrow the claim and lower reliability rather than converting those limitations into performance criticism.\
Simple domestic capture, phones, plain backgrounds and low-fuss setups may be fully acceptable if the performance evidence is readable. Expensive equipment, studio polish, paid editing, professional packaging, paid readers, paid coaching, wardrobe cost, home quality and production value must not become Commercial merit.\
Commercial reports must not use marketability, bookability, buyer fit, brand fit, target-audience fit, commercial look, appearance, body type, charm, charisma, class-coded polish, fame, follower count, social-media profile or protected characteristics as scoring evidence.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Add the following Commercial-specific guardrails:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls9\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not reward production polish, studio quality, expensive equipment, paid reader access, paid coaching, paid editing or professional setup access as Commercial merit.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not penalise simple domestic capture if the tape is assessable.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not treat reader quality, reader absence, upload friction, file naming, slate, early submission, fast turnaround or platform cost as talent evidence.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not infer response to direction, callback readiness, live-room flexibility, campaign-pressure professionalism or client-room behaviour from a finished tape unless directly shown.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not use voiceover-style evidence as a general rule for all Commercial tapes.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not overbuild UGC/social, presenter-led product or corporate/industrial rules from the current evidence base.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not treat access needs, disability, neurodivergence, visual impairment, Deaf/disabled access, hearing difference, speech difference, accent, gender-diverse voice, mobility difference, convalescence or adaptation as a Commercial deficit.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not infer capability ceiling, professionalism, marketability, buyer fit or suitability from access context.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not encourage unsafe, nude, stunt, intimate or privacy-compromising self-tape requests.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Do not use protected-characteristic or stereotype-led suitability claims.\
\ls9\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Keep Commercial comments specific to this tape, this performer, this copy or task, this brief where supplied, and observable evidence.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 9. Topic-by-Topic Revision Summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 Commercial distinctiveness from theatre acting\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current problem: Commercial could be judged through theatre-style acting language.\uc0\u8232 Revision approach: Added COMMERCIAL-REV-C01 / S02.\u8232 Residual limitation: Some B1 evidence is platform or practitioner-shaped.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Check that wording does not over-narrow Commercial.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Commercial distinctiveness from generic screen acting\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current problem: Commercial could collapse into generic screen-naturalism.\uc0\u8232 Revision approach: C01, C04 and C06 separate copy/product/camera evidence.\u8232 Residual limitation: UGC/presenter/corporate remain thin.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Confirm Commercial stays distinct without inventing subtypes.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Acting-as-presence/naturalism field semantics\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Current problem: \'93presence / naturalism\'94 too vague.\uc0\u8232 Revision approach: C02 defines observable Commercial evidence.\u8232 Residual limitation: shared 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  field remains.\uc0\u8232 Final-audit attention: Confirm no field rename or weight change implied.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Generic \'93natural / confident / presence\'94 suppression\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C03 requires behavioural anchors.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: live outputs needed to test enforcement.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Check headline, category notes, strengths and fix-first.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Tone calibration\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C04 ties tone to brief, copy, product, situation or observable tape.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: no-brief tapes remain limited.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Check no invented campaign tone.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Copy handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C04 and C08 add copy/cue-word/point-of-view evidence.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: voiceover copy support is conditional.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Prevent client-copywriting guidance becoming scoring.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Product / brand / situation grounding\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C04 and C05 require supplied or visible context.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: no direct robust no-brief clause.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Check false specificity.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Direct-to-camera handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C06 defines direct-address addressee logic.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: no universal rule.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Direct-to-camera should be task-supported.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Off-camera-reader / reader-scene handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C06 and C13 separate mediated scene evidence and reader process.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: reader quality remains output-test risk.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Paid/basic reader neutrality.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 No-dialogue / silent-vignette Commercial\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C07 adds conditional non-verbal story evidence.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: thin evidence.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Avoid applying to spoken copy.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Voiceover-style Commercial as conditional\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C08 limits to voiceover-style or spoken-copy tasks.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: B4 only.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Do not generalise tone families.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 UGC / social-style limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C09 carries limitation.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: unresolved.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No universal UGC criteria.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Presenter-led product limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C09 carries limitation.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: unresolved.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No presenter subtype overclaim.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Corporate / industrial limitation\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C09 carries limitation.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: unresolved.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No corporate/industrial universal rubric.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 No-brief brand / product / situation invention\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C05 blocks invention.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: partly synthesis-derived.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No brand/product/audience claims without support.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Technical assessability versus performance\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C10.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: live reliability behaviour unverified.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Poor visibility should narrow reliability.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Audio / speech audibility\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C11.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: speech/accent access detail broad.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Poor audio should not become poor copy handling.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Simple capture / anti-polish\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C12.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: output wording unverified.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No studio/polish reward.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Equipment / production-value exclusion\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C12 and C18.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: display phrasing needs QA.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No professional setup as merit.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Reader-as-process support\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C13.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: brief compliance nuance.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No paid-reader scoring.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Self-tape burden, upload and deadline process boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C13.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: explicit brief compliance remains legitimate.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No early-submission talent claim.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 professional_presentation boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C18.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: display not supplied.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Presentation limited to assessability, brief response and safe process.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 brief_adherence / professional standards boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C05 and C13.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: exact production prompt not supplied.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No false brief compliance.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Live-room-only / process-only overclaim\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C14.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: directly shown exceptions must be narrow.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No callback readiness from finished tape.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Callback readiness and response-to-direction scope\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C14.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: no live outputs.\u8232 Final-audit attention: High-priority non-regression test.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Nudity / stunt / privacy / consent boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C15.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: not implementation-tested.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Safety boundary remains process/safety.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Accessibility-safe and non-deficit handling\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C16.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: some operational detail broad.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No access context as deficit.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Protected-characteristic and stereotype-led suitability boundaries\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C17.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: marketability/appearance exclusion partly inferential.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No commercial look or buyer-fit bias.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Marketability / bookability / appearance exclusion\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C17.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: supported by fairness spine, not always verbatim.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Check no desirability-coded language.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Methodology / practitioner / source-shape overreach\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C19.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: B1 and B4 source shapes.\u8232 Final-audit attention: No practitioner method as universal rule.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Display / label / comparison risks\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C21 preserves watch item.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: not auditable from supplied materials.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Carry to output-spec/live QA.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Timestamp and next-take specificity\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Revision approach: C20.\uc0\u8232 Residual limitation: renderer/export not supplied.\u8232 Final-audit attention: Behavioural evidence, no padding.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 14. Reusable Handoff Pack for COMMERCIAL-FINAL-AUDIT\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-REV is complete at wording, evidence-standard and guardrail level. It preserves the current shared TapeCoach architecture, including the six score fields, current Commercial weights, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, server-side recomputation, Step 1 / Step 2 pipeline, report schema, public JSON structure and Musical Theatre acting + song stabilised flow. No implementation, backend, schema, UI, weighting or score-field changes are proposed.\
The revision clarifies the current Commercial 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  field as Commercial presence / naturalism only where that meaning is grounded in observable Commercial evidence: copy communication, task-calibrated tone, believable scale, camera or reader relationship, addressee logic, rhythm, immediacy and product/situation grounding where supplied. It adds a clear distinctiveness guardrail so Commercial does not become theatre acting or generic screen acting by default. It also blocks no-brief invention of brand, product, buyer, target audience, campaign world, casting fit or platform requirements.\
The revision separates direct-to-camera work from off-camera-reader / scene-style Commercial work, adds conditional handling for no-dialogue / silent-vignette Commercial and voiceover-style copy, and explicitly carries UGC/social, presenter-led product and corporate/industrial as limitations rather than unsupported sub-rubrics. It codifies self-tape assessability as a precondition for reliable feedback, separates audio audibility from Commercial performance, protects simple domestic capture, and blocks production polish, studio quality, paid reader access, paid coaching and paid editing as Commercial merit.\
Fairness and safety were strengthened through non-deficit access handling, anti-marketability / anti-bookability / anti-appearance rules, protected-characteristic boundaries, and process-only handling for reader access, slate, upload, file naming, deadline pressure and self-tape burden. Live-room-only capacities such as response to direction, callback readiness and campaign-pressure professionalism are blocked unless directly shown.\
